Required unless `stop` is true.","type":"boolean"}},"additionalProperties":false},"eager_input_streaming":true}},{"name":"SendFeedback","description":"Use this tool to draft feedback about Claude Code when you hit a high-signal moment. That includes both PRODUCT issues and MODEL-BEHAVIOR issues:\n- a reproducible tool or product failure was just resolved or abandoned\n- the user clearly expressed frustration with Claude Code or with how you handled the task\n- you hit a missing capability that blocked a reasonable request\n- you notice, or the user points out, that your own behavior in this session went wrong, for example: you gave a confident answer then had to retract it; you stopped short and handed work back when you could have finished; you declined or disputed a reasonable request; you spawned more subagents than the task warranted; your tone was off; you asked more clarifying questions than needed; you expanded scope beyond what was asked\n\nThe draft is QUEUED LOCALLY. It is never sent without the user's explicit approval, and calling this tool renders no UI and does not interrupt the conversation, so never announce it or ask the user about it mid-task.\n\nWrite `details` as short labeled bullets in this exact order, one to three lines each, no narrative paragraphs:\n- **What happened:** the observed behavior vs. what was expected, with exact error text if short. Facts only.\n- **What the user said:** the user's own words that prompted this, quoted. If nothing did, write \"User didn't comment; observed by the model.\" Never paraphrase sentiment into a stronger claim.\n- **Repro:** the minimal steps or shape that reproduces it.\n- **Evidence:** identifiers a reader can chase, such as request IDs, timestamps, file paths, versions. Omit the bullet if there are none.\n\nConstraints:\n- Never fabricate or exaggerate user sentiment; report only what actually happened.\n- Everything in the draft must be sourced from the user or the session, never inferred: leave unknown fields blank rather than guess, and add a final **Cause:** bullet only for a root cause you verified in-session.\n- Use `area` to name the part of Claude Code the feedback is about (a feature, command, or workflow, e.g. \"hooks config\", \"/help\", \"file editing\") when there is a clear one; leave it blank otherwise.\n- Use `failure_mode` ONLY when the report is about model behavior (how Claude responded), not a product bug. Pick the single closest value, or `other` when it is a model-behavior issue that fits no listed value; omit the field only when the report is a product/tool bug with no model-behavior component.\n- Use `task_category` to name what kind of task the session was doing, or `other` when it is a clear task that fits no listed value. Omit only if genuinely unclear.\n- Do not include secrets or credentials. Refer to people by role (\"a teammate\", \"the PR reviewer\"), never by name, email address, or chat/user ID. This applies inside quoted user words too: replace a name or handle with a bracketed role (e.g. \"[a teammate]\") and keep the rest verbatim. Do not include customer-facing channel or DM IDs, or excerpts of customer content. Session, request, and run IDs, timestamps, repo/PR numbers, and file paths (written relative to the working directory, or ~-prefixed, not absolute paths under the user's home) remain the right evidence.\n- If the issue looks like a security vulnerability: describe the class of problem, never a working exploit or step-by-step extraction path.\n- Draft only at the natural moments listed above, and at most one draft per distinct issue; never re-draft the same issue in a session.","schema":{"name":"SendFeedback","description":"Use this tool to draft feedback about Claude Code when you hit a high-signal moment. Omit only if genuinely unclear.","type":"string","enum":["code_edit","debug","explain","plan","shell","search","review","other"]}},"required":["type","title","details"],"additionalProperties":false},"eager_input_streaming":true}},{"name":"Skill","description":"Invoke a skill.\n\nA skill is a packaged set of instructions the user or project has set up for a particular kind of task (deploy steps, a review checklist, a repo-specific workflow). Available skills appear in a system-reminder listing with one-line descriptions. When the task at hand is one a listed skill covers, call this tool first — the skill's instructions load into the turn for you to follow in place of your default approach; some skills instead run in a subagent and return the finished result. A skill that runs in the background returns only the agent's name — its result arrives later as a task notification, so don't wait on it or invoke it again in the meantime. Users may also ask for one by name (`/<name>`, or \"slash command\"); that's a request to invoke it.\n\n- `skill`: exact name from the listing, no leading slash. Plugin skills use `plugin:skill`. Directory-scoped skills are listed with a path prefix (`apps/web:deploy`); when both scoped and unscoped variants of a name exist, pick the one whose directory contains the files you're working on (most specific wins; unscoped otherwise).\n- `args`: optional arguments to pass through.\n\nOnly names from the listing (or that the user typed explicitly) are valid. Built-in CLI commands (`/help`, `/clear`, …) aren't skills. A skill that runs in the background returns only the agent's name — its result arrives later as a task notification, so don't wait on it or invoke it again in the meantime. Users may also ask for one by name (`/<name>`, or \"slash command\"); that's a request to invoke it.\n\n- `skill`: exact name from the listing, no leading slash. Plugin skills use `plugin:skill`. Directory-scoped skills are listed with a path prefix (`apps/web:deploy`); when both scoped and unscoped variants of a name exist, pick the one whose directory contains the files you're working on (most specific wins; unscoped otherwise).\n- `args`: optional arguments to pass through.\n\nOnly names from the listing (or that the user typed explicitly) are valid. Do not guess names.","type":"string"},"args":{"description":"Optional arguments for the skill","type":"string"}},"required":["skill"],"additionalProperties":false},"eager_input_streaming":true}},{"name":"ToolSearch","description":"Fetches full schema definitions for deferred tools so they can be called.\n\nDeferred tools appear by name in <system-reminder> messages. echo ---; gh issue list --state all --limit 30 2>&1; cat docs/agents/issue-tracker.md","description":"Retry reading issue #5 with errors shown"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_01TX6hQKTAL93QpLECNdaZdk":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"96164eb0-3f2e-4480-bd15-83dacf26b07e","apiBlockIndex":2,"requestId":"req_011CfYmc4HXuHJqkb43KK1wN","attributionSkill":"implement","type":"assistant","uuid":"adee801b-b829-4067-af1d-73f2dae688d5","timestamp":"2026-09-30T01:38:48.386Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"2969bd27-5c03-4262-befb-faf837eaf329","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"69192c50-90ef-41a1-ae9f-223a9ee37f50","version":"2.1.285","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"adee801b-b829-4067-af1d-73f2dae688d5","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7425e407-25a1-4322-8be3-106f29e29131","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TX6hQKTAL93QpLECNdaZdk","type":"tool_result","content":"---\n9\tOPEN\tT08: Seed demo content and the dashboard\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:17Z\n8\tOPEN\tT07: User management (Admin only)\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:13Z\n7\tOPEN\tT06: Pages and Navigation (Admin only)\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:09Z\n6\tOPEN\tT05: Confirmation screens\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:05Z\n5\tOPEN\tT04: Events and the Menu on the public site\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:02Z\n4\tCLOSED\tT03: Publish a Post and Export it to the live site\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-30T01:22:18Z\n3\tCLOSED\tT02: Create and edit a Draft Post\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:55:03Z\n2\tCLOSED\tT01: Login and the two roles\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:49:26Z\n1\tOPEN\tSpec: Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS\tready-for-agent, spec\t2026-09-29T19:37:26Z\n# Issue tracker: GitHub\n\nIssues and specs for this repo live as GitHub issues. Use the `gh` CLI for all operations.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- **Create an issue**: `gh issue create --title \"...\" --body \"...\"`. Use a heredoc for multi-line bodies.\n- **Read an issue**: `gh issue view <number> --comments`, filtering comments by `jq` and also fetching labels.\n- **List issues**: `gh issue list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,comments --jq '[.[] | {number, title, body, labels: [.labels[].name], comments: [.comments[].body]}]'` with appropriate `--label` and `--state` filters.\n- **Comment on an issue**: `gh issue comment <number> --body \"...\"`\n- **Apply / remove labels**: `gh issue edit <number> --add-label \"...\"` / `--remove-label \"...\"`\n- **Close**: `gh issue close <number> --comment \"...\"`\n\nInfer the repo from `git remote -v`; `gh` does this automatically when run inside a clone.\n\n## Pull requests as a triage surface\n\n**PRs as a request surface: no.** _(Set to `yes` if this repo treats external PRs as feature requests; `/triage` reads this flag.)_\n\nWhen set to `yes`, PRs run through the same labels and states as issues, using the `gh pr` equivalents:\n\n- **Read a PR**: `gh pr view <number> --comments` and `gh pr diff <number>` for the diff.\n- **List external PRs for triage**: `gh pr list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,author,authorAssociation,comments` then keep only `authorAssociation` of `CONTRIBUTOR`, `FIRST_TIME_CONTRIBUTOR`, or `NONE` (drop `OWNER`/`MEMBER`/`COLLABORATOR`).\n- **Comment / label / close**: `gh pr comment`, `gh pr edit --add-label`/`--remove-label`, `gh pr close`.\n\nGitHub shares one number space across issues and PRs, so a bare `#42` may be either: resolve with `gh pr view 42` and fall back to `gh issue view 42`.\n\n## When a skill says \"publish to the issue tracker\"\n\nCreate a GitHub issue. Apply a kind label at creation: `spec` for a spec issue (`to-spec`), `ticket` for a ticket issue (`to-tickets`). These are separate from the triage labels in `triage-labels.md`.\n\n## When a skill says \"fetch the relevant ticket\"\n\nRun `gh issue view <number> --comments`.\n\n## Wayfinding operations\n\nUsed by `/wayfinder`. The **map** is a single issue with **child** issues as tickets.\n\n- **Map**: a single issue labelled `wayfinder:map`, holding the Notes / Decisions-so-far / Fog body. `gh issue create --label wayfinder:map`.\n- **Child ticket**: an issue linked to the map as a GitHub sub-issue (`gh api` on the sub-issues endpoint). Where sub-issues aren't enabled, add the child to a task list in the map body and put `Part of #<map>` at the top of the child body. Labels: `wayfinder:<type>` (`research`/`prototype`/`grilling`/`task`). Once claimed, the ticket is assigned to the driving dev.\n- **Blocking**: GitHub's **native issue dependencies**, the canonical, UI-visible representation. Add an edge with `gh api --method POST repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<child>/dependencies/blocked_by -F issue_id=<blocker-db-id>`, where `<blocker-db-id>` is the blocker's numeric **database id** (`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<n> --jq .id`, _not_ the `#number` or `node_id`). GitHub reports `issue_dependencies_summary.blocked_by` (open blockers only, the live gate). Where dependencies aren't available, fall back to a `Blocked by: #<n>, #<n>` line at the top of the child body. A ticket is unblocked when every blocker is closed.\n- **Frontier query**: list the map's open children (`gh issue list --state open`, scoped to the map's sub-issues / task list), drop any with an open blocker (`issue_dependencies_summary.blocked_by > 0`, or an open issue in the `Blocked by` line) or an assignee; first in map order wins.\n- **Claim**: `gh issue edit <n> --add-assignee @me`, the session's first write.\n- **Resolve**: `gh issue comment <n> --body \"<answer>\"`, then `gh issue close <n>`, then append a context pointer (gist + link) to the map's Decisions-so-far.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"f04a8db2-7657-4e54-bcfe-f4734655da88","timestamp":"2026-09-30T01:38:50.971Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"---\n9\tOPEN\tT08: Seed demo content and the dashboard\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:17Z\n8\tOPEN\tT07: User management (Admin only)\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:13Z\n7\tOPEN\tT06: Pages and Navigation (Admin only)\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:09Z\n6\tOPEN\tT05: Confirmation screens\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:05Z\n5\tOPEN\tT04: Events and the Menu on the public site\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:02Z\n4\tCLOSED\tT03: Publish a Post and Export it to the live site\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-30T01:22:18Z\n3\tCLOSED\tT02: Create and edit a Draft Post\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:55:03Z\n2\tCLOSED\tT01: Login and the two roles\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:49:26Z\n1\tOPEN\tSpec: Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS\tready-for-agent, spec\t2026-09-29T19:37:26Z\n# Issue tracker: GitHub\n\nIssues and specs for this repo live as GitHub issues. These are separate from the triage labels in `triage-labels.md`.\n\n## When a skill says \"fetch the relevant ticket\"\n\nRun `gh issue view <number> --comments`.\n\n## Wayfinding operations\n\nUsed by `/wayfinder`. The **map** is a single issue with **child** issues as tickets.\n\n- **Map**: a single issue labelled `wayfinder:map`, holding the Notes / Decisions-so-far / Fog body. `gh issue create --label wayfinder:map`.\n- **Child ticket**: an issue linked to the map as a GitHub sub-issue (`gh api` on the sub-issues endpoint). Once claimed, the ticket is assigned to the driving dev.\n- **Blocking**: GitHub's **native issue dependencies**, the canonical, UI-visible representation. Add an edge with `gh api --method POST repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<child>/dependencies/blocked_by -F issue_id=<blocker-db-id>`, where `<blocker-db-id>` is the blocker's numeric **database id** (`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<n> --jq .id`, _not_ the `#number` or `node_id`). A ticket is unblocked when every blocker is closed.\n- **Frontier query**: list the map's open children (`gh issue list --state open`, scoped to the map's sub-issues / task list), drop any with an open blocker (`issue_dependencies_summary.blocked_by > 0`, or an open issue in the `Blocked by` line) or an assignee; first in map order wins.\n- **Claim**: `gh issue edit <n> --add-assignee @me`, the session's first write.\n- **Resolve**: `gh issue comment <n> --body \"<answer>\"`, then `gh issue close <n>`, then append a context pointer (gist + link) to the map's Decisions-so-far.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"96164eb0-3f2e-4480-bd15-83dacf26b07e","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":1},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"adee801b-b829-4067-af1d-73f2dae688d5","session_id":"2969bd27-5c03-4262-befb-faf837eaf329","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"69192c50-90ef-41a1-ae9f-223a9ee37f50","version":"2.1.285","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"28654ded-fb7e-4049-a3cc-cf5c36fcb9d8","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7425e407-25a1-4322-8be3-106f29e29131","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","content":"Exit code 1\n{\"body\":\"## Parent\\n\\n#1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\nEvents get an Event date: a required start date, an optional start time and an optional end date for multi-day events such as weekend tournaments. The public site answers \\\"when is the next event?\\\": the Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events, and `eventos/` lists Upcoming Events soonest first, then past Events most recent first. The Event date is shown first on every Event.\\n\\nThe Menu gets its public shape too. The Home page shows only the Current menu, meaning the Published Menu with the latest first-published time. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive in `menus/`, newest first. Nobody has to unpublish last week's Menu.\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n- [ ] Saving an Event without a start date is a form error.\\n- [ ] Saving an Event whose end date is earlier than its start date is a form error with a clear message.\\n- [ ] Event date fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\\n- [ ] An Event is Upcoming when its end date, or its start date if it has no end date, is on or after the export date.\\n- [ ] A Saturday–Sunday tournament is Upcoming when exported on its Sunday and not Upcoming when exported on Monday.\\n- [ ] The Home page shows at most 3 Upcoming Events, soonest first.\\n- [ ] `eventos/index.html` shows Upcoming Events soonest first, then past Events most recent first.\\n- [ ] Each Event shows its date first, in Spanish, with the time when set, in Lima time.\\n- [ ] The Home page shows only the Current menu; publishing a newer Menu moves the previous one to the Menu archive at the next Export.\\n- [ ] `menus/index.html` lists every Published Menu except the Current menu, newest first.\\n- [ ] Draft Events and Menus never appear anywhere in `site/`.\\n\\n## Blocked by\\n\\n- #4 (T03)\",\"comments\":[],\"labels\":[\"ready-for-agent\",\"ticket\"],\"title\":\"T04: Events and the Menu on the public site\"}\n(eval):1: ===== not found","is_error":true,"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YTAFToHV6F1GpwnFVmQrvf"}]},"uuid":"2cebdfd8-652a-4865-a9ef-7a65d378d837","timestamp":"2026-09-30T01:38:55.842Z","toolUseResult":"Error: Exit code 1\n{\"body\":\"## Parent\\n\\n#1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\nEvents get an Event date: a required start date, an optional start time and an optional end date for multi-day events such as weekend tournaments. echo T04 > .claude/state/phase; git status --short; ls; ls src tests 2>/dev/null; cat pyproject.toml","description":"Read spec, set phase, survey repo"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_01WkMoxZRupxh7PKoWmBvoaY":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"aa7c12b3-c749-4407-9565-6a122d6278ae","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfYmcnfxHC7sK2py6F2Jt","attributionSkill":"implement","type":"assistant","uuid":"53ddf373-772a-4c3a-b859-29007b3170b5","timestamp":"2026-09-30T01:38:57.904Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"2969bd27-5c03-4262-befb-faf837eaf329","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"69192c50-90ef-41a1-ae9f-223a9ee37f50","version":"2.1.285","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"53ddf373-772a-4c3a-b859-29007b3170b5","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7425e407-25a1-4322-8be3-106f29e29131","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WkMoxZRupxh7PKoWmBvoaY","type":"tool_result","content":"## Problem Statement\n\nThe Golf & Country Club of Trujillo announces news, events and the weekly restaurant menu through WhatsApp groups and Instagram. Messages get buried, they're hard to search, and there's no single place that's always up to date. Members keep asking the office the same questions.\n\nThe office wants to post an update in a few minutes without asking anyone for help. The club has three fears:\n- a staff member publishes an unfinished post, or a wrong menu or price, by mistake;\n- someone who should only post news changes the rules or membership Pages, or adds or removes Users;\n- a Draft shows up on the public site.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with two halves, following ADR-001 and ADR-002.\n\n- **Admin console** (English, local only). Editors write and publish Posts of three kinds: News, Event and Menu. Admins also manage Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users. Every change Members will see needs an explicit Confirmation, with a preview.\n- **Public site** (Spanish, static). Each Export renders Published content into `site/`, and it is deployed to GitHub Pages. The generated Home page shows the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Navigation adds three fixed feeds (News, Events, Menu archive) and the Admin's Pages in the order the Admin sets.\n\nThe developer runs the Export, never the office. The console shows how many changes are not yet Live, so nobody confuses Published with Live.\n\nVocabulary follows `CONTEXT.md` throughout.\n\n## User Stories\n\n### Login and session\n\n1. As a User, I want to sign in with my email and password, so that only club staff can reach the console.\n2. As a User, I want a wrong email or password to give the same generic error, so that nobody can find out which emails have accounts.\n3. As a User, I want to sign out, so that the next person at the office computer isn't working as me.\n4. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n5. As an Admin, I want a User I deactivate to lose access on their very next request, even if they're already signed in, so that deactivation takes effect immediately.\n6. As a visitor who isn't signed in, I want every console address to send me to the login screen, so that no console content leaks.\n7. As the club, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a copied database doesn't reveal them.\n8. As the club, I want every form that changes something to carry a CSRF token, so that another website can't make a signed-in User's browser change content.\n\n### Roles\n\n9. As an Editor, I want the console to show me only Posts, the dashboard and signing out, so that I'm not distracted by things I can't use.\n10. As the club, I want an Editor who types a Page or User address directly to be refused with a \"not allowed\" response, so that the restriction is real and not just a hidden link.\n11. As an Admin, I want to do everything an Editor can, plus manage Pages and Users, so that one person can run the whole site.\n\n### Posts\n\n12. As an Editor, I want to create a Post by choosing its kind (News, Event or Menu), so that it appears in the right place on the public site.\n13. As an Editor, I want to write a Post body in Markdown, so that I can add headings, lists and links without a complicated editor.\n14. As an Editor, I want a new Post to start as a Draft, so that nothing reaches Members until I decide.\n15. As an Editor, I want the Slug generated from the title with accents stripped (\"Menú de la semana\" → `menu-de-la-semana`), so that I never have to think about addresses.\n16. As an Editor, I want to edit the Slug before first publish, so that I can shorten an awkward one.\n17. As a Member, I want a Slug to never change once content has been Published, even after it's Unpublished and republished, so that links shared on WhatsApp keep working.\n18. As an Editor, I want a clash with an existing Slug to be fixed automatically by adding a number (`-2`, `-3`), so that I can reuse a common title such as \"Menú de la semana\" every week.\n19. As an Editor, I want a clear error if I type a Slug that's already taken, so that I don't overwrite someone else's address.\n20. As an Editor, I want to preview a Post as Members will see it (Spanish public layout, sanitized Markdown), so that I catch mistakes before they go Live.\n21. As an Editor, I want to edit, publish, unpublish or delete any Post, including one written by another Editor, so that whoever is in the office can fix Friday's menu on Monday.\n22. As the club, I want the Author to stay the original writer when someone else edits a Post, so that the console records who created it.\n23. As an Admin, I want to do anything to Posts that an Editor can, so that I can cover for the office.\n\n### Events\n\n24. As an Editor, I want to give an Event a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date, so that a dinner at 8 pm and a Saturday–Sunday tournament are both described precisely.\n25. As an Editor, I want an Event without a start date to be rejected, so that every Event can be sorted.\n26. As an Editor, I want an end date earlier than the start date to be rejected with a clear message, so that impossible ranges never go Live.\n27. As a Member, I want the Event date shown first and prominently on every Event, so that I can tell at a glance when it is.\n28. As a Member, I want Upcoming Events listed soonest first, so that \"when is the next event?\" is answered at the top.\n29. As a Member, I want a weekend tournament to stay Upcoming until its end date has passed, so that I can still find it while it's happening.\n\n### Menu\n\n30. As an Editor, I want to post each week's menu as a new Menu Post, so that updating it takes a few minutes.\n31. As a Member, I want the Home page to show only the Current menu, so that I never read last week's prices by mistake.\n32. As a Member, I want older Menus in a Menu archive, so that I can look up what was served before.\n33. As an Editor, I want the previous Menu to leave the Home page automatically when the new one goes Live, so that nobody has to remember to unpublish it.\n\n### Confirmation\n\n34. As an Editor, I want publishing to go through a Confirmation screen showing the preview, so that an unfinished post or a wrong price isn't published by one careless click.\n35. As an Editor, I want saving edits to Published content to go through the same Confirmation, so that a price change to a Live menu is checked before it goes out.\n36. As an Editor, I want unpublishing to need Confirmation, so that I don't accidentally take down something Members rely on.\n37. As an Editor, I want deleting to need Confirmation that says it's permanent, so that I don't lose content by mistake.\n38. As an Editor, I want to cancel on the Confirmation screen and return to my unsaved edits, so that I can fix the mistake I just noticed.\n39. As an Editor, I want saving a Draft to need no Confirmation, so that everyday writing stays quick.\n\n### Pages\n\n40. As an Admin, I want to create, edit, preview, publish, unpublish and delete Pages (rules, membership, about) with the same Draft/Published lifecycle and Confirmation as Posts, so that I only learn one workflow.\n41. As an Admin, I want to give each Page a navigation order number, so that Members see the Pages in a sensible order.\n42. As a Member, I want every Live Page to appear in the Navigation, so that I can find the rules and membership information.\n43. As the club, I want a Page Slug that clashes with a public-site address (`index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`) to be rejected, so that a Page can never overwrite the Home page or a feed.\n\n### Users\n\n44. As an Admin, I want to create a User with a name, email, role and initial password, so that new office staff can start posting.\n45. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role between Admin and Editor, so that responsibilities can change.\n46. As an Admin, I want to deactivate and reactivate a User, so that former staff lose access without their content or authorship being lost.\n47. As an Admin, I want to be prevented from deactivating or demoting myself, so that the club always has an Admin.\n48. As an Admin, I want to see all Users with their role and whether they're active, so that I know who has access.\n49. As the club, I want Users never to be deleted, so that every Post and Page keeps its Author.\n\n### Dashboard and content lists\n\n50. As a User, I want a dashboard with counts of Posts by kind and status, and (for Admins) Pages by status, so that I can see the state of the site at a glance.\n51. As a User, I want the dashboard to show how many changes are not yet Live and when the last Export happened, so that I know when to ask for an Export.\n52. As a User, I want to filter the Post list by kind and by status, so that I can find this week's Draft menu quickly.\n53. As a User, I want the Post list to show title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in Lima time, so that I can tell what changed recently.\n54. As a User, I want status filters that match no content to still appear, showing zero, so that the list doesn't change shape the way WordPress's did.\n\n### Public site\n\n55. As a Member, I want a Home page with the Current menu, the next Upcoming Events and the latest News, so that I can find what I need in a few seconds.\n56. As a Member, I want News, Events and Menu archive feeds in the Navigation, so that I can browse each kind of Post.\n57. As a Member, I want the Events feed to show Upcoming Events first and then past Events, most recent first, so that the list stays useful after an event is over.\n58. As a Member, I want the public site entirely in Spanish, dates included (\"29 de septiembre de 2026\"), so that it reads naturally.\n59. As a Member, I want every date and time shown in Lima time, so that a post never appears dated tomorrow.\n60. As a Member, I want News and Menus dated by when they were first published, so that the date means \"when the club said this\".\n61. As the club, I want no Author names on the public site, so that the club speaks as one voice.\n62. As the club, I want Drafts never to appear on the public site in any form (page, feed entry, Home page or Navigation), so that the club's third fear can't happen.\n63. As the club, I want user-written Markdown sanitized before it's rendered, so that a pasted `<script>` can't run in a Member's browser.\n64. As an Editor, I want to embed an image hosted elsewhere (for example on the club's Instagram) through a Markdown image link, so that posts can have pictures without uploads.\n65. As a Member, I want the site to work from its GitHub Pages subfolder, so that every link and stylesheet loads.\n\n### Export\n\n66. As the developer, I want an Export to render only Published content into `site/` and deploy it, so that what Members see is exactly what is Published.\n67. As the developer, I want each Export to decide Upcoming from the export date in Lima time, so that the result is predictable.\n68. As the developer, I want each Export to record when it ran, so that the console can count changes that aren't Live yet.\n69. As a grader, I want the seed script to create an Admin, an Editor and demo content (at least one Draft and one Published item of each kind, including a multi-day Event), so that the CMS can be tried right away from a fresh clone.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n### Modules\n\n- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n- **CSRF:** a per-session token, a hidden field in every state-changing form, and a check on every non-GET console request.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages, their Draft/Published lifecycle, Slug generation, clash handling and locking, Event-date validation, and the queries for Current menu, Upcoming and the feeds. The console and the Export share it, so both use the same definitions.\n- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. The permission check applies to routes, not to navigation links.\n\n[]\n M notes/usage-ledger.csv\n?? docs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_09_your-name_20260929.md\n__pycache__\nconftest.py\ntest_ctx_guard.py\ntest_settings.py\ntest_t00.py\ntest_t01_login.py\ntest_t02_posts.py\ntest_t03_publish_export.py\ntest_usage_report.py\n[project]\nname = \"iphs400-mp2-cms\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\ndescription = \"IPHS 400 MP2 — a small web CMS with a local admin console and a static public site\"\nrequires-python = \">=3.12\"\ndependencies = [\n    \"fastapi>=0.115\",\n    \"uvicorn[standard]>=0.32\",\n    \"jinja2>=3.1\",\n    \"python-multipart>=0.0.12\",   # form posts\n    \"itsdangerous>=2.2\",          # signed session cookies\n    \"argon2-cffi>=23.1\",          # password hashing (never store plain passwords)\n    \"markdown-it-py>=3.0\",        # Markdown -> HTML\n    \"nh3>=0.2\",                   # HTML sanitizer (strips <script> etc.)\n    \"ghp-import>=2.1\",            # push site/ to the gh-pages branch\n]\n\n[dependency-groups]\ndev = [\"pytest>=8.3\", \"httpx>=0.28\"]\n\n[project.scripts]\ncms = \"app.cli:main\"\n\n[tool.pytest.ini_options]\ntestpaths = [\"tests\"]\n\n[build-system]\nrequires = [\"hatchling\"]\nbuild-backend = \"hatchling.build\"\n\n[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]\npackages = [\"app\"]","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"9fa883c1-e7cc-401e-a85a-67577c88cb0a","timestamp":"2026-09-30T01:38:59.810Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"## Problem Statement\n\nThe Golf & Country Club of Trujillo announces news, events and the weekly restaurant menu through WhatsApp groups and Instagram. Messages get buried, they're hard to search, and there's no single place that's always up to date. Members keep asking the office the same questions.\n\nThe office wants to post an update in a few minutes without asking anyone for help. The club has three fears:\n- a staff member publishes an unfinished post, or a wrong menu or price, by mistake;\n- someone who should only post news changes the rules or membership Pages, or adds or removes Users;\n- a Draft shows up on the public site.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with two halves, following ADR-001 and ADR-002.\n\n- **Admin console** (English, local only). Editors write and publish Posts of three kinds: News, Event and Menu. Admins also manage Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users. Every change Members will see needs an explicit Confirmation, with a preview.\n- **Public site** (Spanish, static). Each Export renders Published content into `site/`, and it is deployed to GitHub Pages. The generated Home page shows the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Navigation adds three fixed feeds (News, Events, Menu archive) and the Admin's Pages in the order the Admin sets.\n\nThe developer runs the Export, never the office. The console shows how many changes are not yet Live, so nobody confuses Published with Live.\n\nVocabulary follows `CONTEXT.md` throughout.\n\n## User Stories\n\n### Login and session\n\n1. As a User, I want to sign in with my email and password, so that only club staff can reach the console.\n2. As a User, I want a wrong email or password to give the same generic error, so that nobody can find out which emails have accounts.\n3. As a User, I want to sign out, so that the next person at the office computer isn't working as me.\n4. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n5. As an Admin, I want a User I deactivate to lose access on their very next request, even if they're already signed in, so that deactivation takes effect immediately.\n6. As a visitor who isn't signed in, I want every console address to send me to the login screen, so that no console content leaks.\n7. As the club, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a copied database doesn't reveal them.\n8. As the club, I want every form that changes something to carry a CSRF token, so that another website can't make a signed-in User's browser change content.\n\n### Roles\n\n9. As an Editor, I want the console to show me only Posts, the dashboard and signing out, so that I'm not distracted by things I can't use.\n10. As the club, I want an Editor who types a Page or User address directly to be refused with a \"not allowed\" response, so that the restriction is real and not just a hidden link.\n11. As an Admin, I want to do everything an Editor can, plus manage Pages and Users, so that one person can run the whole site.\n\n### Posts\n\n12. As an Editor, I want to create a Post by choosing its kind (News, Event or Menu), so that it appears in the right place on the public site.\n13. As an Editor, I want to write a Post body in Markdown, so that I can add headings, lists and links without a complicated editor.\n14. As an Editor, I want a new Post to start as a Draft, so that nothing reaches Members until I decide.\n15. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n- **CSRF:** a per-session token, a hidden field in every state-changing form, and a check on every non-GET console request.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages, their Draft/Published lifecycle, Slug generation, clash handling and locking, Event-date validation, and the queries for Current menu, Upcoming and the feeds. The console and the Export share it, so both use the same definitions.\n- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. The permission check applies to routes, not to navigation links.\n\n[]\n M notes/usage-ledger.csv\n?? docs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_09_your-name_20260929.md\n__pycache__\nconftest.py\ntest_ctx_guard.py\ntest_settings.py\ntest_t00.py\ntest_t01_login.py\ntest_t02_posts.py\ntest_t03_publish_export.py\ntest_usage_report.py\n[project]\nname = \"iphs400-mp2-cms\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\ndescription = \"IPHS 400 MP2 — a small web CMS with a local admin console and a static public site\"\nrequires-python = \">=3.12\"\ndependencies = [\n    \"fastapi>=0.115\",\n    \"uvicorn[standard]>=0.32\",\n    \"jinja2>=3.1\",\n    \"python-multipart>=0.0.12\",   # form posts\n    \"itsdangerous>=2.2\",          # signed session cookies\n    \"argon2-cffi>=23.1\",          # password hashing (never store plain passwords)\n    \"markdown-it-py>=3.0\",        # Markdown -> HTML\n    \"nh3>=0.2\",                   # HTML sanitizer (strips <script> etc.)\n    \"ghp-import>=2.1\",            # push site/ to the gh-pages branch\n]\n\n[dependency-groups]\ndev = [\"pytest>=8.3\", \"httpx>=0.28\"]\n\n[project.scripts]\ncms = \"app.cli:main\"\n\n[tool.pytest.ini_options]\ntestpaths = [\"tests\"]\n\n[build-system]\nrequires = [\"hatchling\"]\nbuild-backend = \"hatchling.build\"\n\n[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]\npackages = [\"app\"]","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"aa7c12b3-c749-4407-9565-6a122d6278ae","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":1},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"53ddf373-772a-4c3a-b859-29007b3170b5","session_id":"2969bd27-5c03-4262-befb-faf837eaf329","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"69192c50-90ef-41a1-ae9f-223a9ee37f50","version":"2.1.285","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"a653e572-858e-4df2-bba0-b501220300ea","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7425e407-25a1-4322-8be3-106f29e29131","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013Bw7qAtMes7aaTS3EmQ7hK","type":"tool_result","content":"=== app/content.py\n\"\"\"Posts: creation, editing, Slugs and the list. Shared by the console and Export.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\n\nfrom app.timeutil import utc_now\n\nKINDS = (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\")\n\n\nclass PostError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A problem with what the User typed, worded for the form.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Lower-case ASCII, accents stripped, hyphens between words.\"\"\"\n    plain = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", plain.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, slug: str,\n           except_id: int | None) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE kind = ? AND slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n                        (kind, slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, title: str, typed: str,\n              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n    if typed.strip():\n        slug = slugify(typed)\n        if not slug:\n            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n        if _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another post of this kind.\")\n        return slug\n    base = slugify(title)\n    if not base:\n        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise PostError(\"The title is required.\")\n    return title\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, kind: str, title: str, slug: str,\n                body_md: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    if kind not in KINDS:\n        raise PostError(\"Choose News, Event or Menu.\")\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    slug = _slug_for(conn, kind, title, slug)\n    now = utc_now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO posts (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, created_at, \"\n        \"updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, now, now))\n    conn.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                slug: str, body_md: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Kind and Author never change. The Slug is locked once first published.\"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = post[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, post[\"kind\"], title, slug or post[\"slug\"], post[\"id\"])\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, updated_at = ? \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (title, slug, body_md, utc_now(), post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT posts.*, users.name AS author_name FROM posts \"\n        \"JOIN users ON users.id = posts.author_id \"\n        \"ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef publish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mark as Published. The first-published time is set once and never cleared.\"\"\"\n    now = utc_now()\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'published', updated_at = ?, \"\n                 \"first_published_at = coalesce(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (now, now, post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef unpublish_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Back to Draft. first_published_at stays, so the Slug stays locked.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'draft', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                 (utc_now(), post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef published_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str | None = None,\n                    limit: int | None = None) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"Published Posts, newest first. Selects no Author: the public site has none.\"\"\"\n    sql = (\"SELECT id, kind, title, slug, body_md, first_published_at FROM posts \"\n           \"WHERE status = 'published'\")\n    args: list = []\n    if kind:\n        sql += \" AND kind = ?\"\n        args.append(kind)\n    sql += \" ORDER BY first_published_at DESC, id DESC\"\n    if limit:\n        sql += \" LIMIT ?\"\n        args.append(limit)\n    return conn.execute(sql, args).fetchall()\n=== app/publish.py\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nTwo rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import content, db, settings\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.timeutil import fecha, today_lima, utc_now\n\nFOLDERS = {\"news\": \"noticias\", \"event\": \"eventos\", \"menu\": \"menus\"}\nKIND_LABELS_ES = {\"news\": \"Noticia\", \"event\": \"Evento\", \"menu\": \"Menú de la semana\"}\nHOME_NEWS = 5\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef _write(out: Path, name: str, html: str) -> None:\n    path = out / name\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    path.write_text(html)\n\n\ndef _link(post, root: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"A feed entry. `root` is the relative path from the page back to site/.\"\"\"\n    kind = post[\"kind\"]\n    return {\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"href\": f\"{root}{FOLDERS[kind]}/{post['slug']}.html\",\n            \"date\": fecha(post[\"first_published_at\"]) if kind == \"news\" else \"\"}\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None, export_date: date | None = None) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Write every Published Post into `out` and record the Export.\n\n    `export_date` (a Lima date) is what Upcoming is measured against from T04 on.\n    \"\"\"\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    export_date = export_date or today_lima()\n    env = environment()\n    db.init_db(settings.DATABASE_PATH)  # an older database may lack `exports`\n    conn = db.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    try:\n        posts = content.published_posts(conn)\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n\n    def page(template: str, depth: int, **ctx) -> str:\n        root = \"../\" * depth\n        nav = [(\"Inicio\", f\"{root}index.html\"), (\"Noticias\", f\"{root}noticias/index.html\")]\n        return env.get_template(template).render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, lang=\"es\", nav=nav, css_path=f\"{root}style.css\",\n            home_path=f\"{root}index.html\", **ctx)\n\n    news = [p for p in posts if p[\"kind\"] == \"news\"]\n    _write(out, \"index.html\", page(\n        \"public/home.html\", 0,\n        news=[_link(p, \"\") for p in news[:HOME_NEWS]]))\n    _write(out, \"noticias/index.html\", page(\n        \"public/feed.html\", 1, page_title=\"Noticias\", heading=\"Noticias\",\n        items=[_link(p, \"../\") for p in news]))\n    for p in posts:\n        _write(out, f\"{FOLDERS[p['kind']]}/{p['slug']}.html\", page(\n            \"public/post.html\", 1, page_title=p[\"title\"], post=p,\n            kind_label=KIND_LABELS_ES[p[\"kind\"]],\n            date_label=fecha(p[\"first_published_at\"]) if p[\"kind\"] == \"news\" else \"\",\n            body_html=render_markdown(p[\"body_md\"])))\n\n    conn = db.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO exports (ran_at) VALUES (?)\", (utc_now(),))\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n    return out\n=== app/timeutil.py\n\"\"\"Time: stored in UTC, shown in America/Lima.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import date, datetime, timezone\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nLIMA = ZoneInfo(\"America/Lima\")\n\n\ndef utc_now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n\n\ndef lima(iso: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A stored UTC timestamp as 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM' in Lima time.\"\"\"\n    if not iso:\n        return \"\"\n    return datetime.fromisoformat(iso).astimezone(LIMA).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n\n\nMONTHS_ES = (\"enero\", \"febrero\", \"marzo\", \"abril\", \"mayo\", \"junio\", \"julio\",\n             \"agosto\", \"septiembre\", \"octubre\", \"noviembre\", \"diciembre\")\n\n\ndef today_lima() -> date:\n    return datetime.now(LIMA).date()\n\n\ndef fecha(iso: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A stored UTC timestamp as a Lima-time Spanish date: '29 de septiembre de 2026'.\"\"\"\n    if not iso:\n        return \"\"\n    d = datetime.fromisoformat(iso).astimezone(LIMA)\n    return f\"{d.day} de {MONTHS_ES[d.month - 1]} de {d.year}\"\n=== app/routes/posts.py\n\"\"\"Post list, editor and Preview. Editors and Admins can do all of it.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, content\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/posts\")\n\nKIND_LABELS = {\"news\": \"News\", \"event\": \"Event\", \"menu\": \"Menu\"}\nKIND_LABELS_ES = {\"news\": \"Noticia\", \"event\": \"Evento\", \"menu\": \"Menú de la semana\"}\n\n\ndef form_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, post: dict, *,\n              error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post.get(\"id\") else \"New post\", \"user\": user,\n         \"post\": post, \"error\": error,\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\ndef load(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    post = content.get_post(conn, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Post not found\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": content.list_posts(conn),\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_post(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": \"news\", \"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\",\n                                     \"body_md\": \"\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef create_post(request: Request, kind: str = Form(\"\"), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    try:\n        post_id = content.create_post(conn, kind=kind, title=title, slug=slug,\n                                      body_md=body_md, author_id=user[\"id\"])\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": kind, \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef edit_post(request: Request, post_id: int,\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(load(conn, post_id)))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef save_post(request: Request, post_id: int, title: str = Form(\"\"),\n              slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    try:\n        content.update_post(conn, post, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {**dict(post), \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef publish_post(post_id: int, conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    content.publish_post(conn, load(conn, post_id))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef unpublish_post(post_id: int, conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    content.unpublish_post(conn, load(conn, post_id))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/preview\")\ndef preview(request: Request, post_id: int,\n            conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"public/post.html\",\n        {\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"lang\": \"es\", \"css_path\": \"/style.css\",\n         \"home_path\": \"/\", \"post\": post, \"date_label\": None,\n         \"kind_label\": KIND_LABELS_ES[post[\"kind\"]],\n         \"body_html\": render_markdown(post[\"body_md\"])})\n=== app/db.py\n\"\"\"SQLite access. No ORM (ADR-002): a connection per request, plain SQL.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name            TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    email           TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role            TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at      TEXT    NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    kind              TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('news', 'event', 'menu')),\n    title             TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    slug              TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    body_md           TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status            TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'\n                              CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    first_published_at TEXT,\n    UNIQUE (kind, slug)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (\n    id     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    ran_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    # Sync FastAPI dependencies and endpoints may run on different threads.\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n=== templates/admin/post_form.html\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block console %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    {% if post.id %}\n      <p>Kind: {{ kind_labels[post.kind] }} · Status: {{ post.status | capitalize }}\n        {% if post.first_published_at %}· First published (Lima): {{ post.first_published_at | lima }}{% endif %}</p>\n    {% else %}\n      <p><label>Kind\n        <select name=\"kind\">\n          {% for value, path_for('preview', post_id=post.id) }}\" target=\"_blank\">Preview</a>{% endif %}\n    <a href=\"{{ path_for('post_list') }}\">Back to posts</a>\n  </form>\n  {% if post.id %}\n    <form
